# Keyword index

Alphabetical. Each keyword lists every app that mentions it in its name, aliases, hardware, tasks, capabilities, port or action names; live apps first, then planned, then retired. Singular and plural are one entry (`light, lights`). Words that match more than 25 apps are left out unless they name hardware, a product or a protocol. Search with `grep -i <word> KEYWORDS.md`. An entry that says NOT BUILT YET means no app does it.
